Increasing the epsilon value can therefore cause serious slow-downs in the running time of the intersections checks." }, { "title": "float SoIntersectionDetectionAction::getIntersectionEpsilon (void ) [static]", "content": "Returns the globally set intersection detection distance epsilon value." }, { "title": "void SoIntersectionDetectionAction::setTypeEnabled (SoType type, SbBool enable)", "content": "Sets whether nodes of specific types (including derived objects) should be tested for intersection or not.\n\nSee also\n isTypeEnabled(), setManipsEnabled(), setDraggersEnabled()" }, { "title": "SbBool SoIntersectionDetectionAction::isTypeEnabled (SoType type, SbBool checkgroups = FALSE) const", "content": "Returns whether nodes of specific types are enabled or not. The checkgroups argument can be set to TRUE if you want the return value to reflect whether the node will be implicit enabled/disabled through the settings controlled by the setManipsEnabled() and setDraggersEnabled() functions.\n\nThe default is that all node types are enabled.\n\nNote that derivation checks are not performed - NAME

SoIntersectionDetectionAction - perform intersection detection between shapes in the scene graph

SYNOPSIS

#include <Inventor/actions/SoIntersectionDetectionAction.h>

Inheritance diagram:

SoAction
  |
  +--SoIntersectionDetectionAction

public:
  SoIntersectionDetectionAction(const SbVec3f & extent = SbVec3f(FLT_MAX, FLT_MAX, FLT_MAX));
  virtual ~SoIntersectionDetectionAction();
  SbBool                  isShapeInternalsEnabled() const;
  void                    setShapeInternalsEnabled(SbBool enable);
